### Extraction rules applied

- Authority dispatch: `00 Project Source Guide → applicable Concern Owner → 16 Repository Architecture → normative subordinate mapping → verification source`.
- `16` is used only for repository placement/naming/import/export/single-authority mapping; behavior is retained at the applicable concern owner.
- Supporting rationale/summary, historical audit/change-log material, and current implementation presence are not used to create canonical rows.
- Repeated references to the same semantic capability are normalized to one `semantic_owner + operation` capability row with multiple source traces.
- A deterministic path/file/symbol grammar is used only where `16` explicitly makes that grammar normative. No current-code filename is used as authority.

### Current validated closure — 2026-08-26

The cumulative Ledger rows and stable IDs were retained. Validation history was compacted to the current material corrections so the status section does not duplicate the same counts and authority statements.

- Current universe: **142 capabilities = 99 Application + 43 Agent owner-local operations**, **473 unique structural/runtime rows**, **85 non-Python coverage entries**.
- `16/01` is now the sole exact Agent-operation manifest; the parent 16 duplicate list was removed. The 43-row set, including `tool_routing.resolve_policy_preconditions` and `planning.resolve_default_container`, is unchanged.
- `16/07` now uses one 24-row Port↔Adapter table and includes `OperationalCommandReplayPort`; the former 23-row duplicate Port list was removed.
- StartRun ordering is reconciled: Application preallocates server-owned IDs and materializes the initial binding before Domain guard evaluation; 04 §10.1 owns the same-UoW durable recipe; later checkpoint writes remain separate.
- Sidebar contracts are reconciled: Calendar Sidebar is selected-month Month View; 90 days applies only to generic Upcoming Browse. Browser persistence uses opaque Local API continuation, never Provider raw token.
- P0 Google Installed/Desktop OAuth is `oauth_client_id`-only with PKCE/state/loopback; no `client_secret` provisioning path remains.
- Structural corrections remain cumulative: retired duplicate IDs are not reused; `STR-467..478`, `STR-289`, and migration NPA slot corrections remain in force.
- Open source gaps, unsourced canonical rows, silently inferred rows, and exact structural duplicates are **0** after affected-scope reverse validation. Phase 2 remains **NOT STARTED**.

### 2.3 Non-authoritative / implementation-artifact handling

- Supporting rationale/summary pages are not used to originate ledger rows.

- Repository implementation presence/absence was not used as a completeness criterion.

Published Plan 재검토에서는 `WAITING_APPROVAL \| VERIFYING → PLANNING`을 허용하되 current durable Review disposition이 `REVISE \| RETRIEVE_MORE \| ROUTE_RECONSIDERATION`이고 unresolved in-flight(`EXECUTING \| UNKNOWN_RESULT \| EXECUTED` awaiting verification)와 unresolved MISMATCH가 0이어야 한다. **사용자 Context Adjustment branch**는 `WAITING_APPROVAL`에서만 허용하며 current Plan 존재, 모든 current Action=`PROPOSED\|MODIFIED`, ACTIVE Approval=0, unresolved/in-flight external effect=0, expected Run/Retrieval revision 일치를 요구한다. 이 branch는 `EXCLUDE_EVIDENCE \| RETRIEVE_MORE`를 normalized `ContextAdjustmentV1`으로 same Run Retrieval owner에 전달한다. 같은 UoW에서 아래 Plan supersession child-authority fence를 적용해 current Plan을 `SUPERSEDED`하고 이미 발생한 외부 Write 및 terminal Action fact는 immutable history로 보존한다. 새 Plan revision은 남은 업무만 계획하며 `VERIFIED \| MISMATCH \| FAILED` Action을 재실행 대상으로 복사하지 않는다. 이미 PLANNING인 bounded revision에서는 반복 호출 금지 | CONFIRMED |

Plan 존재 시 모든 미실행 nonterminal Action(`PROPOSED \| MODIFIED \| APPROVED \| EXPIRED`) → `BLOCKED` → ACTIVE Approval `REVOKED` → Plan `CANCELLED` → Run `BLOCKED` 순서. `VERIFIED \| MISMATCH \| FAILED \| REJECTED \| CANCELLED \| DEPENDENCY_BLOCKED` 등 이미 확정된 Action fact는 보존 | CONFIRMED |

| CAP-APP-066 | recovery / resolve_recovery | recovery.resolve_recovery | Domain State Transition Contract + 04 Domain·Database Design | recovery | Application | resolve_recovery | Application Command | application/use_cases/recovery/ | resolve_recovery.py | ResolveRecoveryCommandV1 / ResolveRecoveryResult / ResolveRecoveryHandler | tests/unit/application/use_cases/recovery/test_resolve_recovery.py | Domain State Transition Contract + 04 Domain·Database Design | 16/01 Spec → Code Deterministic Mapping + applicable 16 subordinate | 12-A State Transition Test Matrix + 12 Test Design | UnitOfWork + owner repositories + CommandReceiptRepository/AuditEventRepository where state-changing | ResolveRecovery(RECHECK): RECOVERY_REQUIRED → reason-specific safe target. `RecoveryContextV1`의 required recheck input이 직전 round와 달라지고 reason별 success predicate를 만족해야 한다. `UNKNOWN_RESULT` recovered-to-EXECUTED 또는 `VERIFICATION_MISMATCH`는 VERIFYING, `UNKNOWN_RESULT` resolved-to-FAILED는 `pre_recovery_status`, `CHECKPOINT_MISMATCH \| CONTRACT_VIOLATION`은 검증된 `pre_recovery_status/registered resume target`으로 복귀한다. Same-input/no-new-information이면 `applied=false / NO_PROGRESS`로 RECOVERY_REQUIRED 유지 + ResolveRecovery(ACCEPT_PARTIAL): `VERIFICATION_MISMATCH` Recovery에서만 RECOVERY_REQUIRED → COMPLETED + PARTIAL, `cancel_intent_active=false` 필요. pending `PROPOSED \| MODIFIED \| APPROVED \| EXPIRED` → `CANCELLED`, ACTIVE Approval → `REVOKED`, current Plan → `COMPLETED`; 기존 `VERIFIED \| MISMATCH \| FAILED \| REJECTED \| BLOCKED \| CANCELLED \| DEPENDENCY_BLOCKED` fact는 보존 + ResolveRecovery(CREATE_CORRECTIVE_PLAN): RECOVERY_REQUIRED → PLANNING + current Plan → SUPERSEDED + 새 Plan Revision, `cancel_intent_active=false` 필요. Plan supersession은 아래 child-authority fence를 적용해 같은 UoW에서 old ACTIVE Approval을 REVOKED로 닫고 old child execution authority를 제거한다 + ResolveRecovery(CANCEL): RECOVERY_REQUIRED → CANCELLED; `cancel_intent_active=true`, unresolved `EXECUTING \| UNKNOWN_RESULT \| EXECUTED`(verification 미완료)=0 필요. pending `PROPOSED \| MODIFIED \| APPROVED \| EXPIRED` → `CANCELLED`, ACTIVE Approval → `REVOKED`, current nonterminal Plan → `CANCELLED`; 기존 확정 Action fact는 보존 + ResolveRecovery(FAIL): reason matrix가 FAIL을 허용하고 복구 불가가 확정되며 unresolved external-delivery uncertainty가 0일 때 RECOVERY_REQUIRED → FAILED. pending `PROPOSED \| MODIFIED \| APPROVED \| EXPIRED` → `BLOCKED`, ACTIVE Approval → `REVOKED`, current nonterminal Plan → `CANCELLED`; 기존 확정 Action fact는 보존 | CONFIRMED |

### 4.1 Non-Python / generated artifact closure audit

This subsection is a **coverage index**, not a second semantic-authority ledger. It cross-indexes non-`.py` implementation/runtime artifacts to the existing structural/capability owners so that JSON/JSONL/SQL/Markdown/lock/marker/generated artifacts cannot disappear behind their generator/handler rows. TypeScript/TSX production files are already individually enumerated in the structural ledger. A standalone JSON-Schema file is **not inferred** where the sources define only a typed/versioned schema contract without an explicit file mapping.

**Non-Python audit result:** **85 explicit coverage entries, 85 source-closed, 0 partial/open.** The current authority-cleanup snapshot was rechecked across manifest/config/schema/fixture/migration/prompt/evaluation artifacts. `15` supplies the 21 Prompt semantic identities and `16/01` maps all 21 source files plus manifest/input-contract realization. `13` supplies Evaluation artifact semantics and `16` maps the single current `evaluation/` repository tree, including the exact 12 result filenames. Static fixture scenario instances are not individually enumerated because the current Source authority classifies those concrete filenames as implementation choices/non-closed verification-data identifiers after the exact `.json` grammar is fixed.

Audit exclusions remain deliberate: Production `build-config.json`, generic `config.json`, ambient `.env` authority and an Ollama credential file are explicitly forbidden, so they are not required-artifact rows. A standalone JSON-Schema file is not invented where the sources define only a versioned typed schema without an explicit file mapping. Fingerprinted frontend assets, installer binaries and rotating runtime filenames are generated build/runtime outputs rather than stable canonical identifiers; their integrity/containment is represented by the Release Manifest or the owning wildcard artifact family.

| DEP-001 | Layer dependency direction | React / API / LangGraph → Application → Domain + Ports ← Outbound Adapters | 03 System Architecture; 16/05 | Application may not import concrete Adapter/Provider SDK/SQLite implementation. |
| DEP-002 | Connector I/O call chain | Workflow/FastAPI boundary → Application canonical operation → SignedToolRegistry validated binding → Connector Application Port → Core-side Connector Adapter → ConnectorRuntimeRegistry + MCPClientPort → Connector MCP Server → Provider API | 03; 07; 08; 09; 10; 16/07 | Provider API/SDK and credential application stay inside connector MCP process-side adapters. |
| DEP-003 | Domain mutation UoW | Application lifecycle handler → UnitOfWork → CommandReceiptRepository + owning Repository(ies) + AuditEventRepository → COMMIT | 04; State Contract; 07; 08; 16/07 | Required lifecycle Audit/Receipt and domain mutation are atomic; external I/O is outside SQLite transaction. |
| DEP-004 | Workflow durable continuation | External-control Application handler → lifecycle mutation + WorkflowHandoff(PENDING) in same UoW → run.schedule_run_execution → WorkflowExecutionPort → BackgroundRunExecutorAdapter → exact persisted admission/checkpoint resume | 03; 04; 06; 07; 08; 16/01; 16/06; 16/07 | No direct HTTP→LangGraph bypass; handoff is Application/Workflow control outbox, not lifecycle authority. |
| DEP-005 | Checkpoint separation | Application/Workflow → CheckpointPort → SqliteCheckpointAdapter / LangGraph-managed checkpoint tables | 03; 04; 06; 07; 10; 16/07 | Checkpoint owns resume position, not approval/execution truth. |
| DEP-006 | Retrieval read chain | Retrieval deterministic node → application retrieval.execute_read → RunRetrievalCachePort/ConnectorReadPort → connector adapter/MCP → Provider | 05; 07; 15; 16/01; 16/07 | Raw provider continuation remains only in process-local Run Retrieval Cache entry. |
| DEP-007 | Prompt/LLM runtime | Agent semantic operation → PromptRef/PromptRegistry assembly → StructuredInferencePort → StructuredInferenceRuntimeRouter → API LLM or Ollama adapter/runtime | 06; 10; 15; 16/01; 16/07 | Prompt input is allowlisted typed projection; evaluation Gold/Grader metadata is not runtime input. |
| DEP-008 | Verification/Recovery | Application verification/recovery operation → Connector Read Port → provider reread/search → Domain store/transition | 01-B; 04; State Contract; 07; 08 | Tool response is not final success authority; UNKNOWN_RESULT never creates blind resend/new write attempt. |
| DEP-009 | Local API boundary | React → same-origin FastAPI Route/Schema → Application Handler | 02; 03; 07; 09; 10; 16/01 | Route owns transport only; no Route→SQL/Domain transition/concrete adapter shortcut. |
| DEP-010 | Repository/test ownership | Canonical production capability path → mirrored canonical unit-test path → architecture enforcement closed-set comparison | 12; 16/01; 16/09; 16/12 | Required manifest comes from design mapping, never implementation discovery. |
| DEP-011 | Evaluation isolation | Production runtime must not import evaluation; evaluation consumes product contracts and artifacts through dedicated evaluation roots | 03; 12; 13; 16/05; 16/02 | Evaluation is candidate-comparison authority, not product behavior authority. |
| DEP-012 | Single production authority | One semantic capability → one production owner/path/symbol + intended callers cut over + old live authority zero + canonical tests | 16 Source; 16/08; 16/12 | Phase 1 records this invariant only; it does not perform KEEP/MOVE/DELETE decisions. |

- `GAP-P1-001..009` were all **freshly revalidated against the 2026-08-25 authority-cleanup Source snapshot** and remain resolved.

- No current production code was inspected to resolve any gap.
- Phase state after fresh revalidation: **`PHASE1_CANONICAL_LEDGER_COMPLETE`**.
- Phase 2 implementation analysis remains **NOT STARTED**.
